I don't get annoyed with them. It's not their fault. I don't like it.\n\nDoctor: When you think about the fact that you've let deadlines slip and have had to apologize to others, do you feel an overwhelming sense of hopelessness or despair about your situation?\n\nPatient: No, I wouldn't say hopeless. Just... annoyed with myself, I guess.\n\nDoctor: When you are struggling with these work tasks, do you ever feel a sense of restlessness or find it hard to sit still?\n\nPatient: No... not really. I can sit at my desk just fine. It's just... hard to actually get the work done. But I don't feel restless or anything.\n\nDoctor: When you have to face these work tasks that you are struggling with, do you experience any internal feelings of apprehension or a sense of dread?\n\nPatient: No... not dread.
